Prepared scratch modules are not counted as implemented milestones.


## 5. M5r continuation gate — 2026-10-17

**Status:** accepted on 2026-10-17 after the independent production review and
Windows/Linux functional gates. Not deployed. M6 scratch preparation is not
accepted by this gate.

**Behavior:** public frame/keyframe and MindView carry the mode and Hold spot's
cell, letter and radius, with no ownership or capture clock. Native and Rhai
Standard drive for an idle Hold objective, park inside its square, retry an
unreachable objective and hand an Advance inside the square back to doctrine.
The motor retains its requested goal and goal-keyed Arrived/NoRoute outcome;
Standard consumes that result instead of guessing arrival from distance.
Identical active or completed requests are idempotent; a NoRoute request may
explicitly retry. PLACE invalidates the route while retaining the logical goal,
and a wreck releases its motor before formation assignment reads the table.
The private entry-zone fan assignment survives subsequent goals within the same
zone, so arriving hunters retain their spacing without treating each other as
terrain. It is cleared on ownership/explicit helm changes and leaving that zone.

Shot origins are captured against each actual shot before impact removes it.
Only a blocked/cover miss launched from the current cell teaches an avoid cell;
overlapping flights and movement between launch/impact are covered. Own launch
geometry survives notice redaction without restoring a lost target's facts.

Twelve of the fourteen removed A/F/G/I/K tests are restored through the mind
seam, including the original strong ford-spacing case, native/Twin/Rhai lanes,
three pulse cadences and snapshot/resume suffixes. D/J remain excluded. SUPPORT
still fights what its own view finds; no charge-target knowledge is invented.
M4.5a's optional order-expiry policy remains separate. Hold help, the leader
prompt and the drone description now describe the returned behavior. Drone pack
version 4 changes its note only; its measured digest is
`59a0a26f39a582243de8c88fc6afd61cd5d3ac7ca9ccd336cbfd42b31bf1db50`.

**Compatibility:** snapshot 12; frame 5; API 3; stock 2;
`rhai=1.26.0;api=3;stock=2`. Standard SHA-256 is
`92987a0c26bc442ef87640f026dfea16dc0934739b5788607f4d3322b24e07ab`.
The inventory is 269 registrations (268 distinct), 233 resolution calls and
149 parity getters. Standard has 31 functions and 15 memory keys. No old
snapshot or ABI migration is supplied. Snapshot shape is 559 paths,
`ec7b85a1dbd3b7588a2843377d6da6ac468f2e270675cbf4de37a8d8374262d3`;
the three added nullable motor paths are requested_goal, outcome and fan.

**Reviewed deterministic changes:** the normal replay's 34 lines and J replay's
50 lines retain their event timing/actions/draws, with changed source/hash
identity. Against M5rules the shipped duel also changes ten position summaries
from 15s: Blue #7 now reaches its initial patrol goal before moving on, instead
of treating the adjacent opening cell as arrival. Its first/last J and duel pins
are `d0ee55ca186576de`, `108ca51243c9efb0` and `5585302e6fd5db4c`.
The seated weapon tape changes only its two source identifiers. Nine seal pins
move with the explicit source/ABI identity. The pooled corpus now ends fourteen
matches for red and one for blue; native/Rhai outcomes remain equal. A pure
source simplification restores the existing 1,200-budget two-tank headroom
without changing actions or memory.

**Verification:**

- Initial broad tacsim sweep: 2,252 passed, 15 failed, 12 ignored in 45 reports.
  Failures were investigated as compatibility pins, canonical JSON, snapshot
  version/shape and order fixtures. This original run is not reported as green.
- Nine affected targets after the first repairs: 1,484 passed and three failed.
  Subsequent libraries/content/extensions/conditions/Rhai/Twin run: 1,122 passed,
  zero failed, two ignored. Final order fixtures pass all 43 tests. The affected
  arena (51), cockpit (183), minds (89), motor (42), reflex (85), and weapons (19)
  targets also pass. The final workspace gate and its corrected help rerun are
  recorded below.
- Final Windows cross-platform corpus passes all four pins; 3,600 float-probe
  thinks retain bitmask 369098751. Whole-match native/Rhai equality passes
  15 matches / 30 plays / 10,968 substeps / 9,378 ledger lines, zero divergences.
- Linux final executable behavior already passes shipped-scale (9), normal Twin
  (11, one ignored), whole-match equality with the same counts, and 45 real
  worker-pool matches. The final four-pin Linux rerun passes against the recorded
  expectations, matching Windows exactly.
- Web: 45 suites / 564 tests passed initially; two workers timed out starting
  under host load. Those two suites pass on a two-worker retry (19 tests), for
  all 47 suites / 583 tests covered. Canonical cockpit fixtures come from the
  real Rust builder; their only changes are version 5 and mode/motor/objective.
- Web type checking and production build pass with zero warnings. Observer
  contracts pass 32 tests. The independently built bot passes 706 tests across
  22 reports, with zero failures and five ignored, including real SSH and arena
  end-to-end coverage.
- Fresh release harnesses pass all four mind load variants on Windows and Linux:
  held/sound true, no faults, overflows or failure labels. The ceiling's snapshot
  maximum is 5,240,701 bytes on both hosts. Its p99 wake/window times are
  6.377/13.482 ms on Windows and 12.080/23.219 ms on Linux; CPU is 3.193% and
  8.605%, respectively. These times remain report-only.
- Reviewed 89 deterministic baseline leaves against exact Windows/Linux equality
  of all nine deterministic objects and every mind/cohort block. Only those
  leaves and explicit update provenance changed in the Linux baseline; original
  blessing metadata and historical timings remain intact. The fresh harness
  delta check passes, including 196 mind fields and the CI view. No Windows
  baseline alias is retained.

Timing remains report-only under the owner's ruling. Determinism, bounded work,
faults and overflow checks remain acceptance conditions.


**Final repository gate:** `cargo test --offline --workspace --exclude mush-bot
-j 2 --no-fail-fast` completed 398 reports: 7,506 passed, one failed, 92 ignored.
The sole failure was the help tokenizer treating the prose words Advance and Hold
as standalone verbs because they were in inline code. Their prose formatting was
corrected; the actual freshly built `help_commands` executable then passed all
four tests, including a final repeat after the documentation correction. No
functional test was waived. The separately built bot's 706 passing tests are
recorded above. All-target workspace Clippy with `-D warnings` and locked strict
workspace rustdoc (`RUSTDOCFLAGS=-D warnings`, including private items) passed.
Workspace `cargo fmt --all -- --check` exceeded Windows' command-line length;
the equivalent check was run once for each package from Cargo metadata, all
passing. `git diff --check` passed.

**Independent review:** the reviewer did not author the production changes and
found no actionable defect in motor goal/outcome/fan transitions, native/Rhai
Hold and arrival parity, shot-sequence origin association, redaction, hashing,
snapshot or frame projection. The reviewer authored the restored historical
regression tests and explicitly excluded those tests from the independent-review
claim. Separate baseline parsing confirmed that the ten changed duel position
summaries change unit 7 alone, with event kinds, timestamps, actions and draws
unchanged. Root inspected the restored assertions against the historical cases.

**Continuation:** a fresh fetch during this gate found IC11 at `26e3b1fa` on
origin/main. It changes no M5r file. Rebase before promoting the M6 assembly;
reconcile its shared command/storage registrations with IC11. M7 still waits
for accepted M6 and a fresh economy-stability checkpoint, with its own design
note. Timing remains report-only.

## 6. M6 candidate and acceptance work — 2026-10-17

**Not yet accepted or deployed.** Candidate `34a22ac1` is published on
`codex/unit-minds-through-m6`, after merge `f37ff334` incorporated IC11
`26e3b1fa`. The ten work packages in 17d were integrated into this candidate,
with separate review by file ownership, rather than ten independently landed
commits. The candidate includes authoring and immutable source/revision custody,
generic cards and rules, private traces and retained tapes, proving and bench
experiments, web/TUI controls and signed public-key SSH transport. M7 economic
rights are not implemented by these workflow records.

The fresh Linux candidate at exactly `34a22ac1` passes 1,180 library tests,
the native/Rhai whole-match twin gate and all 45 worker-pool matches. Its real
24-unit Class III, 900-second recording retains 86,376 thinks and 3,600 substeps
in 49,509,957 bytes across 791 chunks. Complete replay checks 21,611 slices and
reaches final hash `b9bc11feacbabcf2` and ledger SHA-256
`2b8df3d1946c59e5add53c1ed296afa21635f9c5bcc6d7aba11b89187c9c8e04`.
Recording/replay took 83.731/37.031 seconds on the VPS; those timings are evidence,
not acceptance thresholds. The final Windows replay is still owed.

Fresh Windows and Linux corpus measurements produce identical four rows, with
all M5r stopping times and the floating-point probe bitmask `369098751` unchanged.
The literal pins still fail until the reviewed M6 identity/hash update lands.
AF1 first exposed a fixture that requested two profiles on a coreless hull;
M6 correctly captured single-profile ROM. Its chosen-profile fixture needs a
real governed core before the new snapshot shape can be measured and pinned.
No shape or behavior value is inferred from a declaration.

The web gate passes 53 suites/609 tests, zero check errors/warnings and a production
build. The full Windows workspace/bot/documentation sweep is still running after
removing stale temporary-assembly Cargo artifacts. Earlier assembly results are
development evidence only. Strict live workspace/all-target Clippy passed before
the latest acceptance-test additions and must cover those additions as well.

Independent acceptance review found four evidence gaps and assigned corrections:
the port's pooled win rate must fall inside the fresh external bot interval;
actual proving must report zero mind faults; an actual retained proving result
must replay through BENCH to its terminal cursor; and two proving accounts must
progress alongside a live arena in one server with bounded work and paired
latency output. Validator correction `8ac8f159` enforces the first two and passes
12 Python tests, retaining a full report on behavioral failure. The latter two
test additions await the Cargo gate. Host timing remains report-only; outcome,
fault, replay, resource and progress checks remain acceptance requirements.

Still required before acceptance: final workspace/bot/rustdoc/Clippy checks,
reviewed snapshot/corpus/harness compatibility pins, the committed-source author
story and fresh external thirty-game comparison, and final independent review.
M7 still requires its separate merged-economy checkpoint and design note. Q-UM-17
is settled: existing sorties finish after parent-licence loss, while new sorties
and filings wait for restoration.
